\displaystyle=(u+\xi)\langle\nabla(\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u),\partial^{m}u\rangle+\underset{A_{51}}{\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\nabla\left(\nabla\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right),(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}\,.

Note that

(u+\xi)\langle\nabla(\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u),\partial^{m}u\rangle=-(u+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using similar arguments as in (
5.43
), we obtain

\displaystyle A_{51}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|^{s-1}_{H^{s}})\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\,.

Thus,

(5.45)

\displaystyle(\mu+\xi)\left\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+A_{5}\lesssim\left(1+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}^{s-1}\right)\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\,.

For the term
A_{6}
,

\displaystyle A_{6}

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\right],\ (1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{A_{61}}{\underbrace{-2\langle\partial^{m}(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta),\partial^{m}u\rangle}}\ \underset{A_{62}}{\underbrace{-2\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{1+\theta})\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta),(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\rangle}}\,.

The term
A_{61}
can be estimated by

\displaystyle A_{61}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\nabla\psi\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\Delta\psi\right\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|u\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and
A_{62}
satisfies

\displaystyle A_{62}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}\left\langle\left|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right|\left|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\left(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\right)\right|,\left|\partial^{m}u\right|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left\|\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left\|u\right\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\left\|u\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

Hence,

(5.46)

\displaystyle A_{6}\lesssim\left(1+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\left(\left\|u\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\,.

For the quantity
A_{7}
, we decompose it as

\displaystyle A_{7}

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\theta}{1+\theta}\nabla\left(\theta+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right],(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{A_{71}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\theta\nabla\theta+\theta\nabla\tilde{g}(U)\right],\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{A_{72}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\left[\theta\nabla\theta+\theta\nabla\tilde{g}(U)\right],(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}\,.

The term
A_{71}
can be further split as

\displaystyle A_{71}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\partial^{m}\theta^{2},\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\rangle-\left\langle\theta\nabla\partial^{m}\tilde{g}(U),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\theta\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\tilde{g}(U),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{A_{711}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\partial^{m}\theta^{2},\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}+\underset{A_{712}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla\theta\partial^{m}\tilde{g}(U),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}\underset{A_{713}}{\underbrace{+\left\langle\theta\partial^{m}\tilde{g}(U),\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{A_{714}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\theta\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\tilde{g}(U),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}\,.

The subterms
A_{71i}
(
1\leq i\leq 4
) can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle A_{711}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\theta\right\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\left\|\nabla\theta^{2}\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\lesssim\left(\left\|\theta\right\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle A_{712}\lesssim\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\partial^{m}\tilde{g}(U)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\left\|u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle A_{713}\lesssim\left\|\theta\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\partial^{m}\tilde{g}(U)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle A_{714}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}\left\langle\left|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\theta\right|\left|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\tilde{g}(U)\right|,\left|\partial^{m}u\right|\right\rangle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\tilde{g}(U)\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\,.

Thus,

\displaystyle A_{71}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}})(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}+\|u\|_{H^{s}})\times(\|\nabla u\|^{2}_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}})\,.

For
A_{72}
, we have

\displaystyle A_{72}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{1+\theta})|,|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}[\theta\nabla\theta+\theta\nabla\tilde{g}(U)]|,|\partial^{m}u|\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla(\frac{1}{1+\theta})\|_{H^{s-1}}(\|\theta\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\theta\nabla\tilde{g}(U)\|_{H^{s-1}})\|\Delta u\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s-1}})(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\tilde{g}(U)\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s-1})(\|\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

Consequently,

(5.47)

\displaystyle A_{7}\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}+\|u\|_{H^{s}})(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2})\,.

Observe that the quantity
A_{8}
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle A_{8}

\displaystyle=-\langle\partial^{m}[\frac{2A}{1+\theta}\nabla\cdot(\triangledown d\odot\triangledown d)],(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{A_{81}}{\underbrace{-2A\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}(\triangledown d\odot\triangledown d),\partial^{m}u\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{A_{82}}{\underbrace{-2A\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{1+\theta})\nabla\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\triangledown d\odot\triangledown d),(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\rangle}}\,.

The term
A_{81}
can be dominated by

\displaystyle A_{81}

\displaystyle=2A\langle\partial^{m}(\nabla d\odot\nabla d),\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\rangle\lesssim\bigl\|\partial^{m}(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)\bigr\|_{L^{2}}\bigl\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\bigr\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla d\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

Moreover, we can control
A_{82}
as follows:

\displaystyle A_{82}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\Bigl(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\Bigr)||\nabla\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)|,|\partial^{m}u|\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\Bigl(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\Bigr)\bigr\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla d\odot\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s-1}}\lesssim(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s-1}})\|\nabla d\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s}})\|\nabla d\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

Hence,

(5.48)

\displaystyle A_{8}\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s}})\|\nabla d\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

Finally, the term
A_{9}
can be estimated by

(5.49)

\displaystyle A_{9}

\displaystyle=-\langle\Delta\partial^{m}\psi,(1+\theta)\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\theta\partial^{m}u+(1+\theta)\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle\leq c\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\|u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\right)+\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

for some constant
c>0
. Substituting the estimates (
5.41
), (
5.42
), (
5.44
), (
5.45
), (
5.46
), (
5.47
), (
5.48
) and (
5.49
) into (
5.39
), and summing over
|m|\leq s
in the resulting inequality, we obtain

(5.50)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|u\|_{H_{1+\theta}^{s}}^{2}+\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq s}\left\langle P^{\prime}(1+\theta)\nabla\partial^{m}\theta,\partial^{m}u\right\rangle-\sum_{|m|\leq s}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\|\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s}}+\|u\|^{s}_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|^{s}_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\|u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,.

Together with the definitions of
\mathbf{E}_{s}(t)
and
\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)
in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
), we deduce estimate (
5.37
) from (
5.50
). This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

5.4.
\theta
-dissipative mechanism

In this subsection, we mainly seek the damping effect of the density
\theta
arising from the
u
-equation in (
4.6
). More precisely, we establish the following lemma.

Lemma 5.6
.

Let
s\geq 3
be an integer. Then one has

(5.51)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|u+\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\|u\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\right)+a\gamma\|\nabla\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle-c_{0}\big[\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}-(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\big]-c_{1}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\mathbf{E}_{s}(t)\right)\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)\,,

where the functionals
\mathbf{E}_{s}(t)
and
\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)
are defined in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
), respectively, the weighted function
\mathfrak{w}(\theta)
is given in (
4.12
), and the constants are
c_{0}=\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}>0
,
c_{1}=\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}>0
.

Proof.

Applying the derivative operator
\partial^{m}
(
|m|\leq s-1
) to equation (
5.38
) and taking the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation with
\nabla\partial^{m}\theta
, we obtain

(5.52)

\displaystyle\underset{B_{1}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}+\underset{B_{2}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}+\underset{B_{3}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\mathcal{P}^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\nabla\theta\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{B_{4}}{\underbrace{+\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{1}{1+\theta}\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle=\underset{B_{5}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\mu}{1+\theta}\Delta u\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}+\underset{B_{6}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\mu+\xi}{1+\theta}\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{B_{7}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\Delta\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}-\underset{B_{8}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{B_{9}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\theta}{1+\theta}\nabla\left((1+\theta)\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}\ \underset{B_{10}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2A}{1+\theta}\nabla\cdot(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}\,.

Using the first equation in (
4.6
), we have

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\langle\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\rangle-\langle\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial_{t}\partial^{m}\theta\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{B_{11}}{\underbrace{\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\langle\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\rangle}}+\underset{B_{12}}{\underbrace{\langle\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\theta)\rangle}}+\underset{B_{13}}{\underbrace{\langle\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial^{m}[(1+\theta)\nabla\cdot u]\rangle}}\,.

Observe that

\displaystyle B_{11}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\partial^{m}u+\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\,,

and

\displaystyle B_{12}

\displaystyle=\underset{B_{121}}{\underbrace{\langle\partial^{m}u,u\cdot\nabla(\nabla\partial^{m}\theta)\rangle}}+\underset{B_{122}}{\underbrace{\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\langle\partial^{m}u,\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\cdot\nabla(\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta)\rangle}}+\underset{B_{123}}{\underbrace{\langle\partial^{m}u,\partial^{m}(\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta)\rangle}}\,.

The terms
B_{12i}
(
1\leq i\leq 3
) can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle B_{121}=-\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\theta,\nabla\cdot(u\otimes\partial^{m}u)\rangle\lesssim\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\cdot(u\otimes\partial^{m}u)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}},

and

\displaystyle B_{122}\lesssim\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\langle|\partial^{m}u|,|\partial^{m^{\prime}}u|\,|\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta|\rangle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,,

and

\displaystyle B_{123}\lesssim\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}(\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

Hence,
B_{12}
satisfies the bound

\displaystyle B_{12}\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

For the term
B_{13}
,

\displaystyle B_{13}

\displaystyle=-\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u,(1+\theta)\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\rangle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\theta\nabla\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\langle|\nabla\partial^{m}u|,|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\theta|\,|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u|\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\,.

Consequently,

(5.53)

\displaystyle-B_{1}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\partial^{m}u+\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}})\|u\|_{H^{s}}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\right)\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

For the term
B_{2}
, we have

(5.54)

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle=-\langle\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\rangle\lesssim\|\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

The term
B_{3}
can be decomposed as

\displaystyle B_{3}=\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}}}+\underbrace{\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C^{m^{\prime}}_{m}\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}[\frac{{P}^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}]\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\rangle}_{B_{31}}\,.

The term
B_{31}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle-B_{31}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\left\langle\left|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{{P}^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta})\right|\,|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta|,|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta|\right\rangle\lesssim\left\|\nabla\left[\frac{{P}^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|^{s}_{H^{s-1}}\right)\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\lesssim\left(\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\theta\right\|^{s}_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\,,

where the penultimate inequality follows from Lemma 3.2 of
[
11
]
. Therefore,

(5.55)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{{P}^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}}}^{2}-B_{3}\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s-1})\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\,.

For the term
B_{4}
, we have

(5.56)

\displaystyle B_{4}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\partial^{m}\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\big(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\big)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\left\langle\left|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right||\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\big(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\big)|,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\||\nabla d|^{2}\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\tilde{g}(U)\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left(\left\||\nabla d|^{2}\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\tilde{g}(U)\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle+\left(\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}^{s}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left(\left\|\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,.

For the term
B_{5}
, we obtain

(5.57)

\displaystyle B_{5}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\frac{\mu}{1+\theta}\Delta\partial^{m}u,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle+\mu\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u,\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|\Delta\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}\,\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\left\langle\left|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right|\,\left|\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right|,\,|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}+C\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\,\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}+C\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s}}\right)\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

for some harmless constant
C>0
. Similarly,

(5.58)

\displaystyle B_{6}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\mu+\xi}{1+\theta}\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq(\mu+\xi)\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}+C\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,,

and

(5.59)

\displaystyle B_{7}\leq\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|\Delta\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\leq\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\,.

For the term
B_{8}
, we have the bound

\displaystyle B_{8}

\displaystyle=-2\left\langle\frac{1}{1+\theta}\partial^{m}(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle-2\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C^{m^{\prime}}_{m}\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{1+\theta})\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial^{m}(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)||\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta)|,|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

(5.60)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|^{s}_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\,.

The term
B_{9}
can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle B_{9}

\displaystyle-\left\langle\frac{1}{1+\theta}\partial^{m}\left[\theta\nabla\left(\theta+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C^{m^{\prime}}_{m}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{1+\theta})\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}[\theta\nabla(\theta+\tilde{g}(U))],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{m}\left[\theta\nabla(\theta+\tilde{g}(U))\right]\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)||\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\left[\theta\nabla(\theta+\tilde{g}(U))\right]|,|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{g}(U)\|_{H^{s-1}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

(5.61)

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{g}(U)\|_{H^{s-1}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{s}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,.

Finally, the term
B_{10}
can be bounded by

(5.62)

\displaystyle B_{10}

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\frac{2A}{1+\theta}\partial^{m}\left[\nabla\cdot(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)\right],\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle-2A\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C^{m^{\prime}}_{m}\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{1+\theta})\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\cdot(\nabla d\odot\nabla d),\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{m}\nabla\cdot(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)||\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\cdot(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)|,|\nabla\partial^{m}\theta|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nabla d\odot\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left\|\nabla d\odot\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left(\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}+\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}^{s}\right)\left\|\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla d\right\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\,.

Substituting the estimates (
5.53
), (
5.54
), (
5.55
), (
5.56
), (
5.57
), (
5.58
), (
5.59
), (
5.4
), (
5.4
) and (
5.62
) into (
5.52
) and summing over
|m|\leq s-1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|u+\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\|u\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\right)+\|\nabla\theta\|^{2}_{H^{s-1}_{\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}}}

\displaystyle\quad-\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\big[\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}-(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}\,\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\big]

(5.63)

\displaystyle\quad-\sup_{t,x}(1+\theta)^{\frac{2-\gamma}{2}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}}}^{2}\right)\,.

Recalling the expression of
\omega(\theta)
in (
4.12
) together with the definitions of
\mathbf{E}_{s}(t)
and
\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)
in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
), we deduce estimate (
5.51
) from (
5.4
). This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

5.5.
Estimates for the deformation gradient tensor
\nabla\psi
in (
4.6
)

In this subsection, we aim to establish an estimate for the
H^{s}
norm of the deformation gradient tensor
\nabla\psi
. From the third equation of (
4.6
), we have

(5.64)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\Delta\psi+\frac{1}{\mu}\Delta\psi+\frac{1}{\mu}\Delta w=-\Delta\left(u\cdot\nabla\psi\right)\,,

where

\displaystyle w=\mu(u-\bar{u})-(\psi-\bar{\psi})\,,\ \bar{u}=\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}^{3}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}u\,\mathrm{d}x\,,\ \bar{\psi}=\frac{1}{|\mathbb{\mathbb{T}}^{3}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\psi\mathrm{d}x\,.

Using the second equation of (
4.6
), the unknown
w
satisfies

(5.65)

\displaystyle-\Delta w=(\mu+\xi)\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)-\nabla P(1+\theta)+F,

where

\displaystyle F=

\displaystyle-\theta\Delta\psi-2\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta-\theta\nabla\left(\theta+\tilde{g}(U)\right)-2A\nabla\cdot\left(\nabla d\odot\nabla d\right)

\displaystyle-(1+\theta)\left(\partial_{t}u+u\cdot\nabla u\right)-\nabla\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\,.

We note that the quantity
w=\mu(u-\bar{u})-(\psi-\bar{\psi})
satisfies the Stokes system

(5.66)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}-\Delta w+\nabla q=F_{incom}\\
\qquad\quad\nabla\cdot w=-\nabla\cdot\psi\end{cases}

in the incompressible model (see (1.18) of
[
9
]
), where the source term
F_{incom}
is controllable and
\nabla\cdot\psi=\mathrm{tr}U
is actually
nonlinear
due to the incompressibility. Unfortunately, the previous mechanism fails in the present work. As shown in (
5.65
), the unknown
w
satisfies the elliptic equation with the source term

(\mu+\xi)\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)-\nabla P(1+\theta)+F\,,

which contains the linear effect
(\mu+\xi)\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)-\nabla\theta
. This linear effect is uncontrollable if we treat
w
using standard elliptic theory. Consequently, we should exploit the relation

-\Delta\psi\sim\partial_{t}u+\nabla\theta-\mu\Delta u+(u+\xi)\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)+\cdots

derived from the second
u
-equation in (
4.6
) to control the quantity
w=\mu(u-\bar{u})-(\psi-\bar{\psi})
. More precisely, we have the following lemma.

Lemma 5.7
.

Let
s\geq 3
be an integer. Then one has

(5.67)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|u-\bar{u}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu}\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{\mu}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+(\mu-\frac{c_{p}}{\mu})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}-\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{\mu+\xi}{\mu}\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\rangle-c_{1}a\gamma(\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{s}{2}}(t)\right)\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)\,,

where the functionals
\mathbf{E}_{s}(t)
and
\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)
are defined in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
), respectively, the weighted function
\mathfrak{w}(\theta)
is given in (
4.12
), the constant
c_{1}
is from Lemma
5.6
, and
c_{p}>0
is the Poincaré constant.

Proof.

Applying the derivative operator
\partial^{m}
(
|m|\leq s
) to equation (
5.64
) and taking the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation with
\partial^{m}\psi
, we obtain

(5.68)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\mu}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=\underset{C_{1}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\Delta\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\psi),\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle}}+\underset{C_{2}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\Delta\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle}}\,.

The term
C_{1}
can be decomposed as

\displaystyle C_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\Delta\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\psi),\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle=-\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\psi),\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\nabla\psi),\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\rangle-\langle\partial^{m}(\nabla u\cdot\nabla\psi),\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{C_{11}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle u\cdot\nabla\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle}}\underset{C_{12}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\cdot\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{C_{13}}{\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\cdot\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle}}\underset{C_{14}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\nabla u\cdot\nabla\psi),\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle}}\,.

The terms
C_{1i}
(
1\leq i\leq 4
) are estimated as follows:

\displaystyle C_{11}=\frac{1}{2}\langle\nabla\cdot u,|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi|^{2}\rangle\lesssim\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{12}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}u||\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi|,|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{13}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}\left\langle|\partial^{m^{\prime}}u||\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi|,|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi|\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{14}\lesssim\|\partial^{m}(\nabla u\cdot\nabla\psi)\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

Consequently,

(5.69)

\displaystyle C_{1}=\langle\Delta\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\psi),\partial^{m}\psi\rangle\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

We now estimate the term
C_{2}=\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\Delta\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\psi\rangle
, which involves the unknown
w
in (
5.68
). Integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
, we obtain

(5.70)

\displaystyle C_{2}=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\Delta\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\Delta\partial^{m}\psi\right\rangle\,.

From the second
u
-equation in (
4.6
), it follows that

\displaystyle-\Delta\psi

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}u+u\cdot\nabla u+\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\nabla\theta+\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\mu}{1+\theta}\Delta u-\frac{\mu+\xi}{1+\theta}\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)+\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla{\psi}\cdot\nabla\theta

(5.71)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\theta}{1+\theta}\nabla\left(\theta+\tilde{g}(U)\right)+\frac{2A}{1+\theta}\nabla\cdot\left(\nabla d\odot\nabla d\right)\,.

Then (
5.70
) and (
5.5
) yield

(5.72)

\displaystyle C_{2}

\displaystyle=\underset{C_{21}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial_{t}\partial^{m}u\rangle}}-\underset{C_{22}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{C_{23}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\nabla\theta\right]\right\rangle}}+\underset{C_{24}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left(\frac{\mu}{1+\theta}\Delta u\right)\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{C_{25}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right]\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{C_{26}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left(\frac{\mu+\xi}{1+\theta}\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)\right)\right\rangle}}\ \underset{C_{27}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\right]\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\underset{C_{28}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\theta}{1+\theta}\nabla\left(\theta+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right]\right\rangle}}\ \underset{C_{29}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2A}{1+\theta}\nabla\cdot\left(\nabla d\odot\nabla d\right)\right]\right\rangle}}\,.

We now handle the terms
C_{2i}
(
1\leq i\leq 9
) one by one. The term
C_{21}
can be computed as

\displaystyle C_{21}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mu(u-\bar{u})-(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u}),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\right\rangle+\left\langle u-\bar{u},\partial_{t}\bar{u}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial_{t}\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\|\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}\partial_{t}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle+\left\langle u-\bar{u},\partial_{t}\bar{u}\right\rangle\,.

Note the cancellation

\displaystyle\langle u-\bar{u},\partial_{t}\bar{u}\rangle=\partial_{t}\bar{u}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}{(u-\bar{u})}\mathrm{d}x=0\,.

Thus,

\displaystyle C_{21}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\left\|\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle\big)-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}\partial_{t}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle\,.

For the inner product
-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}\partial_{t}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle
, we use the third
\psi
-equation of (
4.6
):

\partial_{t}\left(\psi-\bar{\psi}\right)=-\left(u-\bar{u}\right)-u\cdot\nabla\psi+\bar{u\cdot\nabla\psi}

Then, together with the fact
\langle u-\bar{u},\bar{u}\rangle=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}\partial_{t}\left(\psi-\bar{\psi}\right),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u}),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}\left(u\cdot\nabla\psi-\overline{u\cdot\nabla\psi}\right),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u}),\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle u-\bar{u},\bar{u}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}\left(u\cdot\nabla\psi-\overline{u\cdot\nabla\psi}\right),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\|\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}_{{C}_{211}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla\psi),\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}_{{C}_{212}}\ \underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\overline{u\cdot\nabla\psi}\left\langle\mathbf{1},u\right\rangle}_{{C}_{213}}\,.

The term
C_{211}
satisfies

\displaystyle C_{211}=\frac{1}{\mu}\|\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{c_{p}}{\mu}\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

by the Poincaré inequality. For
C_{212}
,

\displaystyle C_{212}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mu}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\mathrm{C}_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi,\partial^{m}u\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle u\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}u\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\psi\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}+\|u\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}+\|u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\psi\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\,,

For
C_{213}
, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle C_{213}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}|^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}u\cdot\nabla\psi\,\mathrm{d}x\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}u\,\mathrm{d}x=-\frac{1}{\mu|\mathbb{T}|^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}(u-\overline{u})\cdot\nabla\psi\,\mathrm{d}x\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}u\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u-\overline{u}\|_{L^{2}}\,\|\nabla\psi\|_{L^{2}}\,\|u\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{2}}\,\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\,\|\nabla\psi\|_{L^{2}}\,,

where we used
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\bar{u}\cdot\nabla\psi\mathrm{d}x=0
and the Poincaré inequality
\|u-\bar{u}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}
. As a result,

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}\partial_{t}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle\leq C\|\nabla u\|^{2}_{H^{s}}+C\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\,,

which implies

(5.73)

\displaystyle C_{21}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\partial^{m}(u-\bar{u})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{p}}{\mu}\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+C\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}

for some constant
C>0
. Moreover, the term
C_{22}
admits the estimate

(5.74)

\displaystyle C_{22}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle\partial^{m}(u-\overline{u}),\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\rangle+\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\overline{\psi}),\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial^{m}(u-\overline{u})\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial^{m}(\psi-\overline{\psi})\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}(u\cdot\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|u-\overline{u}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\psi-\overline{\psi}\|_{H^{s}})\|u\cdot\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\|u\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,,

where we used the Poincaré inequalities
\|u-\bar{u}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}
and
\|\psi-\bar{\psi}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{L^{2}}
.

We now turn to the term
C_{23}
. It can be split as

\displaystyle C_{23}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\nabla\theta\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\nabla\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle-\frac{1}{\mu}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{C_{231}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}w,\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle{\mathbf{1}}_{m\neq 0}}}\ \underset{C_{232}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle w,\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\nabla\theta\right\rangle}}\quad

\displaystyle\quad\underset{C_{233}}{\underbrace{+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\nabla\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\quad\underset{C_{234}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\right\rangle}}

(5.75)

\displaystyle\quad\underset{C_{235}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\right\rangle}}\,.

The terms
C_{23i}
(
1\leq i\leq 5
) are estimated term by term. For
C_{231}
,

(5.76)

\displaystyle C_{231}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\mathbf{1}_{m\neq 0}\left\|\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim a\gamma\left(\mu\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\,.

Similarly,

(5.77)

\displaystyle C_{232}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\left\|\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim a\gamma\left(\mu\|u-\overline{u}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\psi-\overline{\psi}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim a\gamma\left(\mu\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}\,.

The remaining terms are estimated as

(5.78)

\displaystyle C_{233}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{m\neq 0}\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\left(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right)^{\prime}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,,

and

(5.79)

\displaystyle C_{234}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,,

and

(5.80)

\displaystyle C_{235}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\right\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla\left[\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}\right]\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

Substituting the estimates (
5.76
), (
5.77
), (
5.78
), (
5.79
) and (
5.80
) into (
5.5
) yields

(5.81)

\displaystyle C_{23}

\displaystyle\lesssim a\gamma\left(\mu\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla\theta\right\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle+\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}\,.

The quantity
C_{24}
can be decomposed as

\displaystyle C_{24}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left(\frac{\mu}{1+\theta}\Delta u\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\frac{1}{1+\theta}\Delta\partial^{m}u\right\rangle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{C_{241}}{\underbrace{-\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}w,\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}-\underset{C_{242}}{\underbrace{\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underset{C_{243}}{\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underset{C_{244}}{\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right\rangle}}\,.

By Hölder’s inequality and Sobolev embedding, we obtain

\displaystyle C_{241}

\displaystyle=-\langle\nabla\partial^{m}w,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle+\langle\nabla\partial^{m}w,\frac{\theta}{1+\theta}\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}-\mu\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim C\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\left(\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}-\mu\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\rangle\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{242}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{243}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}\left\|\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{244}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}\left\|\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\Delta\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}u\right\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|w\right\|_{H^{s+1}}\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\theta\right\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{H^{s}}\,.

Consequently,

(5.82)

\displaystyle C_{24}\leq-\mu\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle+(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})(\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\,.

For the term
C_{25}
, we split it as

\displaystyle C_{25}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{1}{1+\theta}(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{C_{251}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{1}{1+\theta}(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\right]\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underset{C_{252}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\partial^{m^{\prime}}(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\right\rangle}}{}

\displaystyle\quad\underset{C_{253}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\nabla(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\right\rangle}}\,.

We then obtain

\displaystyle C_{251}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla w\|_{H^{s}}\left\|\frac{1}{1+\theta}(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\right\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(1+\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}})(\||\nabla d|^{2}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{g}(U)\|_{H^{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{252}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U))\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{s+1}}\left\|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{H^{s-1}}\left(\left\||\nabla d|^{2}\right\|_{H^{s}}+\left\|\tilde{g}(U)\right\|_{H^{s}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})\left(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{253}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\partial^{m}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\left(-A|\nabla d|^{2}+\tilde{g}(U)\right)\right\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{s+1}}\left\|\frac{1}{1+\theta}\right\|_{H^{s}}\left(\left\|\nabla d^{2}\right\|_{H^{2}}+\left\|\tilde{g}(U)\right\|_{H^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})\left(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,.

Hence,

(5.83)

\displaystyle C_{25}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})\left(\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,.

By arguments analogous to those in (
5.82
), we obtain

(5.84)

\displaystyle C_{26}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left(\frac{\mu+\xi}{1+\theta}\nabla(\nabla\cdot u)\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-\left(\mu+\xi\right)\left\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\mu+\xi}{\mu}\left\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\right\rangle

\displaystyle+C\left(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left(\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}

for some constant
C>0
.

We now split
C_{27}
as

\displaystyle C_{27}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\cdot\nabla\theta\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underset{C_{271}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}w,\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\,\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}+\underset{C_{272}}{\underbrace{\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right)\partial^{m}\theta\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle\quad\underset{C_{273}}{\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\mu}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{2}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right)\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\right\rangle}}\,.

These terms are bounded as follows:

\displaystyle C_{271}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\partial^{m}\theta\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{272}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{m}w\right\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right)\right\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+1)\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}\,,

and

\displaystyle C_{273}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\end{subarray}}\langle|\partial^{m}w|,|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right)||\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta|\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|=1\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right)\right\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
|m^{\prime}|\geq 2\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m}w\|_{L^{4}}\left\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\theta\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{s+1}}\left\|\frac{1}{1+\theta}\nabla\psi\right\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\,.

Therefore,

(5.85)

\displaystyle C_{27}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\,.

By similar reasoning, the terms
C_{28}
and
C_{29}
can be controlled as

(5.86)

\displaystyle C_{28}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{\theta}{1+\theta}\nabla(\theta+\tilde{g}(U))\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})(\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\,,

and

(5.87)

\displaystyle C_{29}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu}\left\langle\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\left[\frac{2A}{1+\theta}\nabla\cdot(\nabla d\odot\nabla d)\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\,.

Substituting the estimates (
5.73
), (
5.74
), (
5.81
), (
5.82
), (
5.83
), (
5.84
), (
5.85
), (
5.86
) and (
5.87
) into (
5.72
), we obtain

(5.88)

\displaystyle C_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu}\langle\Delta\partial^{m}w,\partial^{m}\psi\rangle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\partial^{m}(u-\overline{u})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\overline{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+(\frac{c_{p}}{\mu}-\mu)\|\nabla\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu+\xi}{\mu}\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\rangle+c_{0}a\gamma(\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s})(\|u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})

\displaystyle\times(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\,.

Inserting the estimates (
5.69
) and (
5.88
) into (
5.68
) and summing over
|m|\leq s
, we deduce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|u-\bar{u}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu}\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{\mu}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+(\mu-\frac{c_{p}}{\mu})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}-\frac{\mu+\xi}{\mu}\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\rangle

(5.89)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle-c_{0}a\gamma(\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}^{s}\right)\left(\|u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\theta\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)\,.

Recalling the definitions of
\mathbf{E}_{s}(t)
and
\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)
in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
), we obtain estimate (
5.67
) from (
5.5
). This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

5.6.
Closing the energy estimates: Proof of Proposition
4.1

We first add equation (
5.7
) to (
5.16
). This yields

(5.90)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(|\bar{d}|^{2}+\|d-\bar{d}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\right)+2A\lambda\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+2A\lambda\|\Delta d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t))\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)\,.

Next, we add (
5.22
) to (
5.37
). It then follows that

(5.91)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\theta\|_{H_{\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}}^{s}}^{2}+\|u\|_{H_{1+\theta}^{s}}^{2}\right)+\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}-\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{s}{2}}(t))\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)\,.

Third, we multiply (
5.51
) by an arbitrary small
\eta\in(0,1)
and add the result to (
5.91
). This gives

(5.92)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\|\theta\|_{H_{\frac{P^{\prime}(1+\theta)}{1+\theta}}^{s}}^{2}+\|u\|_{H_{1+\theta}^{s}}^{2}+\eta\|u+\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\eta\|u\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\eta\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+a\gamma\eta\|\nabla\theta\|_{H_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}^{s-1}}^{2}-\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle

\displaystyle-c_{0}\eta\left(\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{s}{2}}(t))\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)\,.

Fourth, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
and
\epsilon>0
, we multiply (
5.67
) by
\delta
and add the resulting relation together with
\epsilon
times (
5.92
) to (
5.90
). This yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\epsilon a\gamma\|\theta\|_{H_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}^{s}}^{2}+\epsilon\|u\|_{H_{1+\theta}^{s}}^{2}+\epsilon\eta\|u+\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\epsilon\eta\|u\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}-\epsilon\eta\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+|\bar{d}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|d-\bar{d}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\delta\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\delta\|u-\bar{u}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}-\frac{2\delta}{\mu}\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\partial^{m}(\psi-\bar{\psi}),\partial^{m}u\rangle)

\displaystyle\quad+(\epsilon\mu+\delta\mu-\delta\frac{c_{p}}{\mu})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+(\epsilon+\delta)(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\epsilon\eta a\gamma\|\nabla\theta\|_{H_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}^{s}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\mu}\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-(\epsilon+\delta)\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\partial^{m}u\rangle-\frac{\mu+\xi}{\mu}\delta\sum_{|m|\leq s}\langle\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}\psi,\nabla\cdot\partial^{m}u\rangle

(5.93)

\displaystyle\quad-c_{0}\epsilon\eta[\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+(\mu+\xi)\|\nabla\cdot u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}}]\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}

\displaystyle\quad-c_{1}\delta a\gamma(\mu\|\nabla u\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{H^{s}})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{s-1}_{\mathfrak{w}(\theta)}}+2A\lambda\|\nabla d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+2A\lambda\|\Delta d\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{s}{2}}(t))\mathbf{E}_{s}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathbf{D}_{s}(t)\,.

Together with Lemma
4.1
, estimate (
5.6
) implies the bound (
4.26
). This completes the proof of Proposition
4.1
.
